### app\static\js\settings.js ###
/**
 * TypeSphere Unified Settings & Telemetry Manager
 * Distinctly separates Default Settings, Anonymous Session Settings, and User Profile Settings.
 */
class SettingsManager {
 constructor() {
 this.defaultSettings = {
 theme: 'dark',
 font_family: 'JetBrains Mono',
 font_size: 22,
 caret_style: 'smooth',
 sound_enabled: true,
 sound_theme: 'mechanical',
 sound_volume: 0.7,
 show_keyboard: true,
 default_duration: 60,
 default_content: 'words',
 default_level: 'moderate',
 blind_mode: true, // Default: ON
 ghost_mode: false // Default: OFF
 };
 this.current = Object.assign({}, this.defaultSettings);
 this.initSettings();
 }
 initSettings() {
 // Check if user is authenticated via server injection
 const authElement = document.getElementById('user-auth-meta');
 const isAuthenticated = authElement ? authElement.dataset.authenticated === 'true' : false;
 if (isAuthenticated) {
 // Profile Settings: stored in database or localized profile cache
 const stored = localStorage.getItem('typesphere_user_profile_prefs');
 if (stored) {
 try {
 this.current = Object.assign({}, this.defaultSettings, JSON.parse(stored));
 } catch {}
 }
 } else {
 // Anonymous Users: Session settings only. Refreshing restores default settings
 const sessionData = sessionStorage.getItem('typesphere_anon_session_prefs');
 if (sessionData) {
 try {
 this.current = Object.assign({}, this.defaultSettings, JSON.parse(sessionData));
 } catch {}
 } else {
 this.current = Object.assign({}, this.defaultSettings);
 }
 }
 this.applyAll();
 }
 applyAll() {
 // 1. Theme
 document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', this.current.theme);
 // 2. CSS Variables
 document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--font-mono', `"${this.current.font_family}", monospace`);
 document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--typing-font-size', `${this.current.font_size}px`);
 // 3. Caret Style
 const carets = document.querySelectorAll('.caret');
 carets.forEach(c => {
 c.className = `caret caret-${this.current.caret_style}`;
 });
 // 4. Audio Engine
 if (window.soundEngine) {
 window.soundEngine.enabled = this.current.sound_enabled;
 window.soundEngine.theme = this.current.sound_theme;
 window.soundEngine.setVolume(this.current.sound_volume);
 }
 // 5. On-screen Keyboard Toggle
 const kb = document.getElementById('keyboard-container');
 if (kb) {
 kb.style.display = this.current.show_keyboard ? 'flex' : 'none';
 }
 // Sync Engine Defaults if test page active
 if (window.typingEngine) {
 window.typingEngine.durationLimit = this.current.default_duration;
 window.typingEngine.contentType = this.current.default_content;
 window.typingEngine.level = this.current.default_level;
 window.typingEngine.blindModeActive = this.current.blind_mode;
 window.typingEngine.ghostEnabled = this.current.ghost_mode;
 window.typingEngine.updateControlsUI();
 }
 }
 update(key, value) {
 this.current[key] = value;
 this.applyAll();
 const authElement = document.getElementById('user-auth-meta');
 const isAuthenticated = authElement ? authElement.dataset.authenticated === 'true' : false;
 if (isAuthenticated) {
 localStorage.setItem('typesphere_user_profile_prefs', JSON.stringify(this.current));
 // Save to database
 fetch('/settings/api/save', {
 method: 'POST',
 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 body: JSON.stringify(this.current)
 }).catch(() => {});
 } else {
 sessionStorage.setItem('typesphere_anon_session_prefs', JSON.stringify(this.current));
 }
 }
 resetToDefaults() {
 this.current = Object.assign({}, this.defaultSettings);
 sessionStorage.removeItem('typesphere_anon_session_prefs');
 localStorage.removeItem('typesphere_user_profile_prefs');
 this.applyAll();
 // Call server to reset database settings if signed in
 fetch('/settings/api/reset-defaults', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
 alert('Settings successfully restored to system defaults!');
 window.location.reload();
 }
}
window.settingsManager = new SettingsManager();
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 window.settingsManager.initSettings();
});

### app\static\js\tooltips.js ###
/**
 * TypeSphere Real-Time Predictive Tooltip Engine
 * Dynamically computes screen bounds and displays actionable button telemetry on hover.
 */
class TooltipEngine {
 constructor() {
 this.tooltipEl = null;
 this.activeTarget = null;
 this.initDOM();
 this.bindEvents();
 }
 initDOM() {
 let el = document.getElementById('typesphere-tooltip');
 if (!el) {
 el = document.createElement('div');
 el.id = 'typesphere-tooltip';
 document.body.appendChild(el);
 }
 this.tooltipEl = el;
 }
 bindEvents() {
 // Event delegation on mouseover
 document.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
 const target = e.target.closest('[data-tooltip]');
 if (target) {
 this.showTooltip(target);
 }
 }, true);
 document.addEventListener('mouseout', (e) => {
 const target = e.target.closest('[data-tooltip]');
 if (target && target === this.activeTarget) {
 this.hideTooltip();
 }
 }, true);
 document.addEventListener('click', () => {
 this.hideTooltip();
 });
 window.addEventListener('scroll', () => this.hideTooltip(), { passive: true });
 }
 showTooltip(target) {
 this.activeTarget = target;
 const rawContent = target.getAttribute('data-tooltip');
 if (!rawContent) return;
 let title = target.getAttribute('data-tooltip-title') || 'ACTION OUTCOME';
 let body = rawContent;
 this.tooltipEl.innerHTML = `
 <div class="tooltip-header">
 <svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5">
 <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle>
 <line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12" y2="12"></line>
 <line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12.01" y2="8"></line>
 </svg>
 ${title}
 </div>
 <div class="tooltip-body">${body}</div>
 `;
 const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
 const tooltipRect = this.tooltipEl.getBoundingClientRect();
 // Position above by default; flip below if near top of screen
 let top = rect.top - 58;
 let left = rect.left + (rect.width / 2) - 80;
 if (top < 10) {
 top = rect.bottom + 10;
 }
 if (left < 10) {
 left = 10;
 }
 if (left + 280 > window.innerWidth) {
 left = window.innerWidth - 290;
 }
 this.tooltipEl.style.top = `${top}px`;
 this.tooltipEl.style.left = `${left}px`;
 this.tooltipEl.classList.add('visible');
 }
 hideTooltip() {
 this.activeTarget = null;
 if (this.tooltipEl) {
 this.tooltipEl.classList.remove('visible');
 }
 }
}
window.tooltipEngine = new TooltipEngine();

 <script>
 function toggleMobileNav() {
 const nav = document.getElementById('main-nav-links');
 nav.classList.toggle('mobile-open');
 }
 function dismissAlert(btn) {
 const alertBox = btn.closest('.alert-box');
 if (alertBox) {
 alertBox.classList.add('fade-out');
 setTimeout(() => alertBox.remove(), 350);
 }
 }
 document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 const alerts = document.querySelectorAll('.alert-box');
 alerts.forEach(alertBox => {
 setTimeout(() => {
 alertBox.classList.add('fade-out');
 setTimeout(() => alertBox.remove(), 350);
 }, 3500);
 });
 });
 </script>

{% block scripts %}
<script>
function filterTrophies(type) {
 document.querySelectorAll('[id^="filter-"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active-choice'));
 document.getElementById(`filter-${type}`).classList.add('active-choice');
 const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.trophy-card');
 cards.forEach(card => {
 if (type === 'all' || card.dataset.status === type) {
 card.style.display = 'block';
 } else {
 card.style.display = 'none';
 }
 });
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
const rawPoints = {{ data_points|tojson|safe }};
let currentMetric = 'wpm';
function renderSvgChart(metric, color) {
 const container = document.getElementById('analytics-chart');
 if (!container || !rawPoints || rawPoints.length < 2) {
 container.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 3rem; text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted);">Complete at least two tests to populate the progression curve.</div>';
 return;
 }
 const width = container.clientWidth || 800;
 const height = 240;
 const padding = 40;
 const values = rawPoints.map(p => p[metric]);
 const maxVal = Math.max(...values, metric === 'accuracy' ? 100 : 60);
 const minVal = Math.min(...values, 0);
 const getX = (idx) => padding + (idx / (rawPoints.length - 1)) * (width - 2 * padding);
 const getY = (val) => height - padding - ((val - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal || 1)) * (height - 2 * padding);
 let pathD = `M ${getX(0)} ${getY(values[0])}`;
 for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
 pathD += ` L ${getX(i)} ${getY(values[i])}`;
 }
 const svg = `
 <svg width="100%" height="${height}" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" preserveAspectRatio="none">
 <line x1="${padding}" y1="${height - padding}" x2="${width - padding}" y2="${height - padding}" stroke="#30363d" stroke-width="1"/>
 <line x1="${padding}" y1="${padding}" x2="${padding}" y2="${height - padding}" stroke="#30363d" stroke-width="1"/>
 <path d="${pathD}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
 ${values.map((v, i) => `
 <circle cx="${getX(i)}" cy="${getY(v)}" r="4" fill="${color}"/>
 `).join('')}
 </svg>
 `;
 container.innerHTML = svg;
}
function switchChart(metric) {
 currentMetric = metric;
 document.getElementById('btn-chart-wpm').className = metric === 'wpm' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 document.getElementById('btn-chart-acc').className = metric === 'accuracy' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 document.getElementById('btn-chart-con').className = metric === 'consistency' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 if (metric === 'wpm') {
 document.getElementById('chart-view-title').textContent = 'Net WPM Trajectory';
 renderSvgChart('wpm', '#58a6ff');
 } else if (metric === 'accuracy') {
 document.getElementById('chart-view-title').textContent = 'Keystroke Accuracy Stability Curve';
 renderSvgChart('accuracy', '#3fb950');
 } else {
 document.getElementById('chart-view-title').textContent = 'Interval Consistency & Regularity (%)';
 renderSvgChart('consistency', '#d29922');
 }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 switchChart('wpm');
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

 <a href="{{ url_for('typing.practice_page') }}" class="btn" style="border-color:var(--accent); color:var(--accent); padding:0.75rem 1.25rem;">
 Train Weaknesses &rarr;
 </a>
 {% endif %}
 </div>
 </div>
</div>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{{ url_for('static', filename='css/arcade.css') }}">
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script src="https://cdn.socket.io/4.7.2/socket.io.min.js"></script>
<script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/arcade_runtime.js') }}"></script>
<script>
const GAME_SLUG = "{{ game.slug }}";
let selectedPlayMode = 'solo_ai';
let isBlindMode = false;
let isBackspaceAllowed = true;
function setPlayMode(mode, btn) {
 selectedPlayMode = mode;
 document.querySelectorAll('#setup-play-modes .btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active-choice'));
 btn.classList.add('active-choice');
 const roomWrap = document.getElementById('setup-room-code-wrap');
 const oppWrap = document.getElementById('live-opp-wrapper');
 if (roomWrap) roomWrap.style.display = (mode === '1v1_private' || mode === 'multiplayer') ? 'block' : 'none';
 if (oppWrap) oppWrap.style.display = (mode === 'solo_practice') ? 'none' : 'block';
}
function toggleSetupToggle(type) {
 if (type === 'blind') {
 isBlindMode = !isBlindMode;
 const btn = document.getElementById('toggle-blind-btn');
 btn.textContent = isBlindMode ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
 btn.classList.toggle('active-choice', isBlindMode);
 } else if (type === 'backspace') {
 isBackspaceAllowed = !isBackspaceAllowed;
 const btn = document.getElementById('toggle-backspace-btn');
 btn.textContent = isBackspaceAllowed ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
 btn.classList.toggle('active-choice', isBackspaceAllowed);
 }
}
function openPreGameSetup() {
 document.getElementById('game-setup-screen').style.display = 'block';
 document.getElementById('game-active-arena').style.display = 'none';
 document.getElementById('game-results-screen').style.display = 'none';
}
function startArcadeCountdown() {
 const diff = document.getElementById('setup-difficulty').value;
 const objVal = document.getElementById('setup-objective-val') ? document.getElementById('setup-objective-val').value : '60';
 const charMode = document.getElementById('setup-bubble-char-mode') ? document.getElementById('setup-bubble-char-mode').value : 'letters';
 const code = document.getElementById('setup-room-code') ? document.getElementById('setup-room-code').value.trim() : '';
 document.getElementById('game-setup-screen').style.display = 'none';
 document.getElementById('game-results-screen').style.display = 'none';
 document.getElementById('game-active-arena').style.display = 'block';
 window.arcadeEngine.start({
 slug: GAME_SLUG,
 playMode: selectedPlayMode,
 difficulty: diff,
 objectiveVal: objVal,
 blindMode: isBlindMode,
 backspaceAllowed: isBackspaceAllowed,
 charMode: charMode,
 roomCode: code
 });
}
function abortCurrentGame() {
 window.arcadeEngine.stop();
 openPreGameSetup();
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\bubble_pop.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:880px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.25rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0;">🫧 Bubble Pop Arena</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Score: <strong id="bubble-score" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Lives: <strong id="bubble-lives" style="color:var(--danger); font-size:1.25rem;">❤️❤️❤️</strong></span>
 <a href="{{ url_for('games.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&times; Exit</a>
 </div>
 </div>
 <div id="bubble-stage" style="position:relative; width:100%; height:400px; background:var(--bg-secondary); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius); overflow:hidden; outline:none;">
 <div style="position:absolute; top:30px; left:0; right:0; height:2px; background:var(--danger); opacity:0.5;"></div>
 </div>
 <div style="margin-top:0.75rem; font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 Type the word inside any bubble to pop it before it hits the top ceiling.
 </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const BUBBLE_WORDS = ["pop", "float", "drift", "breeze", "light", "wave", "glide", "swift", "clear", "spark", "fresh", "shine", "glow", "soar"];
let bScore = 0;
let bLives = 3;
let bubbles = [];
let spawnTimer = null;
let floatTimer = null;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 spawnTimer = setInterval(createBubble, 1900);
 floatTimer = setInterval(floatBubbles, 40);
 window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
 if (bLives <= 0) return;
 if (e.key.length !== 1 || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
 const char = e.key.toLowerCase();
 const candidate = bubbles.find(b => b.text.startsWith(b.typed + char));
 if (candidate) {
 candidate.typed += char;
 candidate.el.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--success); text-decoration:underline;">${candidate.typed}</span>${candidate.text.slice(candidate.typed.length)}`;
 if (candidate.typed === candidate.text) {
 popBubble(candidate);
 }
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 } else {
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 }
 });
});
function createBubble() {
 const stage = document.getElementById('bubble-stage');
 if (!stage || bLives <= 0) return;
 const text = BUBBLE_WORDS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BUBBLE_WORDS.length)];
 const el = document.createElement('div');
 el.textContent = text;
 el.style.position = 'absolute';
 el.style.left = `${Math.random() * (stage.clientWidth - 100) + 15}px`;
 el.style.bottom = '0px';
 el.style.padding = '0.75rem 1rem';
 el.style.borderRadius = '50px';
 el.style.background = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2)';
 el.style.border = '2px solid var(--accent)';
 el.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
 el.style.fontFamily = 'var(--font-mono)';
 el.style.fontWeight = '700';
 el.style.boxShadow = '0 0 15px var(--accent-glow)';
 stage.appendChild(el);
 bubbles.push({ el, text, typed: '', y: 0, speed: 1.2 + Math.random() });
}
function floatBubbles() {
 const stage = document.getElementById('bubble-stage');
 if (!stage || bLives <= 0) return;
 for (let i = bubbles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
 const b = bubbles[i];
 b.y += b.speed;
 b.el.style.bottom = `${b.y}px`;
 // Reached top ceiling
 if (b.y >= stage.clientHeight - 60) {
 b.el.remove();
 bubbles.splice(i, 1);
 bLives--;
 updateBubbleLives();
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 if (bLives <= 0) {
 clearInterval(spawnTimer);
 clearInterval(floatTimer);
 alert(`Bubbles burst! Final Score: ${bScore} points.`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 break;
 }
 }
 }
}
function popBubble(b) {
 b.el.remove();
 const idx = bubbles.indexOf(b);
 if (idx !== -1) bubbles.splice(idx, 1);
 bScore += (b.text.length * 15);
 document.getElementById('bubble-score').textContent = bScore;
}
function updateBubbleLives() {
 let h = '';
 for (let i = 0; i < bLives; i++) h += '❤️';
 document.getElementById('bubble-lives').textContent = h || '☠️';
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\cipher_hacker.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:820px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0; color:#10b981;">💻 Cipher Terminal Intrusion</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Decrypted: <strong id="cipher-count" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Lockdown: <strong id="cipher-countdown" style="color:var(--danger); font-size:1.25rem;">30s</strong></span>
 <a href="{{ url_for('games.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&times; Abort</a>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Terminal Screen -->
 <div style="background:#05050d; border:2px solid #10b981; border-radius:var(--radius); padding:2rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem; text-align:left; font-family:var(--font-mono); box-shadow:0 0 20px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2);">
 <div style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.85rem; margin-bottom:0.75rem;">// SECURE SYSTEM DECRYPTION ACTIVE:</div>
 <div id="cipher-hash-display" style="font-size:1.8rem; font-weight:800; color:#10b981; word-break:break-all; margin-bottom:0.5rem;">
 0x7FA9B2
 </div>
 </div>
 <input type="text" id="cipher-input" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Enter exact decryption sequence..." style="width:100%; max-width:480px; padding:0.85rem; background:var(--bg-card); border:2px solid #10b981; border-radius:var(--radius-sm); color:#10b981; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:1.2rem; text-align:center; outline:none;">
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const HASHES = ["0x8FA1", "0xDE4B", "0x91C0", "SHA256", "0xAA12", "PORT:443", "0xFF00", "AES-256", "0x00A1", "KEY:994"];
let cDecrypted = 0;
let cTime = 30;
let currentHash = "0x8FA1";
let cTimer = null;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 const input = document.getElementById('cipher-input');
 input.focus();
 loadNewHash();
 cTimer = setInterval(() => {
 cTime--;
 document.getElementById('cipher-countdown').textContent = `${cTime}s`;
 if (cTime <= 0) {
 clearInterval(cTimer);
 alert(`Access denied! Security firewall triggered. Hashes cracked: ${cDecrypted}.`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 }
 }, 1000);
 input.addEventListener('input', () => {
 if (input.value === currentHash) {
 cDecrypted++;
 cTime = Math.min(45, cTime + 4); // Bonus seconds
 document.getElementById('cipher-count').textContent = cDecrypted;
 input.value = '';
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 loadNewHash();
 }
 });
});
function loadNewHash() {
 currentHash = HASHES[Math.floor(Math.random() * HASHES.length)];
 document.getElementById('cipher-hash-display').textContent = currentHash;
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\falling_words.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:880px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.25rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0;">☄️ Falling Words Arcade</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Score: <strong id="game-score" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Lives: <strong id="game-lives" style="color:var(--danger); font-size:1.25rem;">❤️❤️❤️</strong></span>
 <a href="{{ url_for('games.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&times; Exit</a>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Falling Arena Canvas (No typing box needed: type directly on your keyboard) -->
 <div id="game-canvas" tabindex="0" style="position:relative; width:100%; height:400px; background:var(--bg-secondary); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius); overflow:hidden; outline:none; cursor:default;">
 <div id="danger-line" style="position:absolute; bottom:35px; left:0; right:0; height:2px; background:var(--danger); opacity:0.6;"></div>
 <div id="active-stroke-indicator" style="position:absolute; bottom:8px; left:50%; transform:translateX(-50%); font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:0.9rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 Target word: <span id="current-target-match" style="color:var(--accent); font-weight:700;">[Type any falling word]</span>
 </div>
 </div>
 <div style="margin-top:0.75rem; font-size:0.82rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 No input box required: simply strike the keys of any descending word on your keyboard.
 </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const WORD_POOL = [
 "velocity", "cadence", "rhythm", "kinetic", "focus", "flow", "precision", 
 "matrix", "stream", "quantum", "tactile", "circuit", "binary", "system", 
 "engine", "pilot", "balance", "command", "transit", "standard", "channel"
];
let score = 0;
let lives = 3;
let activeWords = []; // [{ id, text, typed: '', el, x, y, speed }]
let spawnInterval = null;
let physicsInterval = null;
let currentTargetWord = null;
let wordsCleared = 0;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 const arena = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
 arena.focus();
 spawnInterval = setInterval(spawnWord, 2000);
 physicsInterval = setInterval(updatePhysics, 40);
 // Global Direct Keystroke Interceptor
 window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
 if (lives <= 0) return;
 if (e.key.length !== 1 || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
 e.preventDefault();
 const char = e.key.toLowerCase();
 // 1. If currently tracking a word, continue typing it
 if (currentTargetWord) {
 const nextExpected = currentTargetWord.text[currentTargetWord.typed.length];
 if (char === nextExpected) {
 currentTargetWord.typed += char;
 updateWordVisual(currentTargetWord);
 if (currentTargetWord.typed === currentTargetWord.text) {
 destroyWord(currentTargetWord);
 currentTargetWord = null;
 updateTargetLabel();
 }
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 return;
 }
 }
 // 2. Otherwise search for any active falling word starting with this letter
 const candidate = activeWords.find(w => w.text.startsWith(char));
 if (candidate) {
 currentTargetWord = candidate;
 currentTargetWord.typed = char;
 updateWordVisual(currentTargetWord);
 if (currentTargetWord.typed === currentTargetWord.text) {
 destroyWord(currentTargetWord);
 currentTargetWord = null;
 }
 updateTargetLabel();
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 } else {
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 }
 });
});
function spawnWord() {
 const container = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
 if (!container || lives <= 0) return;
 const text = WORD_POOL[Math.floor(Math.random() * WORD_POOL.length)];
 const el = document.createElement('div');
 el.className = 'falling-word-node';
 el.textContent = text;
 el.style.position = 'absolute';
 el.style.left = `${Math.random() * (container.clientWidth - 130) + 15}px`;
 el.style.top = '0px';
 el.style.fontFamily = 'var(--font-mono)';
 el.style.fontWeight = '700';
 el.style.fontSize = '1.05rem';
 el.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
 el.style.background = 'var(--bg-card)';
 el.style.padding = '0.3rem 0.65rem';
 el.style.borderRadius = '5px';
 el.style.border = '1.5px solid var(--border-color)';
 container.appendChild(el);
 activeWords.push({
 id: Math.random(),
 text: text,
 typed: '',
 el: el,
 y: 0,
 speed: 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.3
 });
}
function updatePhysics() {
 const container = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
 if (!container || lives <= 0) return;
 for (let i = activeWords.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
 const w = activeWords[i];
 w.y += w.speed;
 w.el.style.top = `${w.y}px`;
 // Word crossed boundary without completion
 if (w.y >= container.clientHeight - 40) {
 w.el.remove();
 if (currentTargetWord && currentTargetWord.id === w.id) {
 currentTargetWord = null;
 updateTargetLabel();
 }
 activeWords.splice(i, 1);
 lives--;
 updateLivesDisplay();
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 if (lives <= 0) {
 concludeGame();
 break;
 }
 }
 }
}
function updateWordVisual(w) {
 const typedPart = w.text.substring(0, w.typed.length);
 const remaining = w.text.substring(w.typed.length);
 w.el.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--success); text-decoration:underline;">${typedPart}</span><span>${remaining}</span>`;
 w.el.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)';
 w.el.style.boxShadow = '0 0 10px var(--accent-glow)';
}
function destroyWord(w) {
 w.el.remove();
 const idx = activeWords.findIndex(item => item.id === w.id);
 if (idx !== -1) activeWords.splice(idx, 1);
 score += (w.text.length * 15);
 wordsCleared++;
 document.getElementById('game-score').textContent = score;
}
function updateTargetLabel() {
 const lbl = document.getElementById('current-target-match');
 lbl.textContent = currentTargetWord ? `[${currentTargetWord.text}]` : '[Type any falling word]';
}
function updateLivesDisplay() {
 let h = '';
 for (let i = 0; i < lives; i++) h += '❤️';
 document.getElementById('game-lives').textContent = h || '☠️';
}
function concludeGame() {
 clearInterval(spawnInterval);
 clearInterval(physicsInterval);
 alert(`Game Over! Final Score: ${score} points (${wordsCleared} words eliminated).`);
 fetch('/games/api/record-score', {
 method: 'POST',
 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 body: JSON.stringify({ game_mode: 'falling_words', score: score, words_typed: wordsCleared })
 }).finally(() => {
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 });
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

 <div>

 <!-- Rendered Words -->
 </div>
 <input type="text" id="racer-input" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Start typing the passage to throttle forward..." style="width:100%; max-width:520px; padding:0.85rem 1.15rem; background:var(--bg-card); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius-sm); color:var(--text-primary); font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:1.1rem; outline:none;">
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const RACE_PASSAGE = "The open highway stretched across the desert floor under a wide expanse of pale morning sky. High velocity demands relaxed control and steady breathing. When the throttle opens every second compounds into pure forward momentum.";
let currentIdx = 0;
let startTime = null;
let botProgress = 0;
let botInterval = null;
let raceEnded = false;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 renderPassage();
 const input = document.getElementById('racer-input');
 input.focus();
 // Bot paces at steady 65 WPM
 botInterval = setInterval(() => {
 if (!startTime || raceEnded) return;
 botProgress += 0.8;
 const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(botProgress));
 document.getElementById('bot-car-bar').style.width = `${pct}%`;
 document.getElementById('bot-track-stat').textContent = `${Math.round(pct * 5)}m / 500m`;
 if (pct >= 100) {
 clearInterval(botInterval);
 if (!raceEnded) {
 raceEnded = true;
 alert("Pace car crossed the finish line first! Keep training your velocity.");
 finishRacerGame();
 }
 }
 }, 200);
 input.addEventListener('input', () => {
 if (raceEnded) return;
 if (!startTime) startTime = performance.now();
 const val = input.value;
 const spans = document.querySelectorAll('#racer-passage-display .char');
 currentIdx = Math.min(val.length, RACE_PASSAGE.length);
 let errors = 0;
 for (let i = 0; i < spans.length; i++) {
 if (i < val.length) {
 if (val[i] === RACE_PASSAGE[i]) {
 spans[i].className = 'char correct';
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char incorrect';
 errors++;
 }
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char';
 }
 }
 const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((currentIdx / RACE_PASSAGE.length) * 100));
 const elapsedMinutes = (performance.now() - startTime) / 60000;
 const wpm = elapsedMinutes > 0 ? Math.round(((currentIdx - errors) / 5) / elapsedMinutes) : 0;
 document.getElementById('player-car-bar').style.width = `${pct}%`;
 document.getElementById('player-track-stat').textContent = `${Math.round(pct * 5)}m / 500m`;
 document.getElementById('racer-speed').textContent = `${wpm} WPM`;
 document.getElementById('racer-pct').textContent = `${pct}%`;
 if (val === RACE_PASSAGE) {
 raceEnded = true;
 clearInterval(botInterval);
 alert(`🏁 Finish Line Reached! You defeated the pace car with ${wpm} WPM!`);
 finishRacerGame();
 }
 });
});
function renderPassage() {
 const container = document.getElementById('racer-passage-display');
 container.innerHTML = '';
 for (let i = 0; i < RACE_PASSAGE.length; i++) {
 const s = document.createElement('span');
 s.className = 'char';
 s.textContent = RACE_PASSAGE[i];
 container.appendChild(s);
 }
}
function finishRacerGame() {
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\whack_a_word.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:820px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0;">🔨 Whack-A-Word Grid</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Whacked: <strong id="mole-score" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Time: <strong id="mole-time" style="color:var(--warning); font-size:1.25rem;">45s</strong></span>
 <a href="{{ url_for('games.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&times; Exit</a>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- 3x3 Mole Grid -->
 <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3, 1fr); gap:1rem; max-width:600px; margin:0 auto 1.5rem;">
 {% for i in range(9) %}
 <div id="hole-{{ i }}" style="height:100px; background:var(--bg-secondary); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius); display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-weight:800; font-size:1.15rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 <!-- Word pops here -->
 </div>
 {% endfor %}
 </div>
 <div style="font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 Type the active illuminated word immediately to strike the mole.
 </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const MOLE_WORDS = ["swift", "strike", "smash", "rapid", "flash", "clack", "click", "punch", "quick", "whack", "power", "turbo"];
let mScore = 0;
let mTime = 45;
let currentActiveWord = "";
let currentActiveHole = null;
let moleTimer = null;
let clockTimer = null;
let typedBuffer = "";
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 clockTimer = setInterval(() => {
 mTime--;
 document.getElementById('mole-time').textContent = `${mTime}s`;
 if (mTime <= 0) {
 clearInterval(clockTimer);
 clearInterval(moleTimer);
 alert(`Time up! You struck ${mScore} moles.`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 }
 }, 1000);
 spawnMole();
 window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
 if (mTime <= 0 || !currentActiveWord) return;
 if (e.key.length !== 1 || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
 const char = e.key.toLowerCase();
 if (currentActiveWord.startsWith(typedBuffer + char)) {
 typedBuffer += char;
 const hole = document.getElementById(`hole-${currentActiveHole}`);
 hole.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--success); text-decoration:underline;">${typedBuffer}</span>${currentActiveWord.slice(typedBuffer.length)}`;
 if (typedBuffer === currentActiveWord) {
 mScore++;
 document.getElementById('mole-score').textContent = mScore;
 hole.style.background = 'var(--success-bg)';
 hole.style.borderColor = 'var(--success)';
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 spawnMole();
 }
 } else {
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 }
 });
});
function spawnMole() {
 clearTimeout(moleTimer);
 typedBuffer = "";
 // Reset previous hole
 if (currentActiveHole !== null) {
 const prev = document.getElementById(`hole-${currentActiveHole}`);
 prev.textContent = '';
 prev.style.background = 'var(--bg-secondary)';
 prev.style.borderColor = 'var(--border-color)';
 }
 currentActiveHole = Math.floor(Math.random() * 9);
 currentActiveWord = MOLE_WORDS[Math.floor(Math.random() * MOLE_WORDS.length)];
 const hole = document.getElementById(`hole-${currentActiveHole}`);
 hole.textContent = currentActiveWord;
 hole.style.background = 'var(--bg-card)';
 hole.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)';
 hole.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
 moleTimer = setTimeout(spawnMole, 2400); // 2.4s window to whack
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\word_blitz.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:760px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0;">⚡ 60-Second Word Blitz</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Words: <strong id="blitz-words" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Multiplier: <strong id="blitz-mult" style="color:var(--warning); font-size:1.25rem;">1x</strong></span>
 <span>Time: <strong id="blitz-clock" style="color:var(--danger); font-size:1.25rem;">60s</strong></span>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Big Central Word Prompt -->
 <div id="blitz-prompt-card" style="background:var(--bg-secondary); border:2px solid var(--accent); border-radius:var(--radius); padding:2.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 <div id="blitz-word" style="font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:2.8rem; font-weight:900; color:var(--text-primary); letter-spacing:0.05em;">
 READY
 </div>
 </div>
 <input type="text" id="blitz-input" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Type the flashing word here..." style="width:100%; max-width:440px; padding:0.85rem; background:var(--bg-card); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius-sm); color:var(--text-primary); font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:1.3rem; text-align:center; outline:none;">
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const BLITZ_CORPUS = ["energy", "fusion", "voltage", "current", "matrix", "stream", "spark", "motion", "dynamo", "thrust", "ignite", "reactor", "hyper", "velocity", "signal", "photon", "laser"];
let bWordsTyped = 0;
let bMultiplier = 1;
let bStreak = 0;
let bTimeRemaining = 60;
let currentBlitzWord = "blitz";
let bStarted = false;
let bInterval = null;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 const input = document.getElementById('blitz-input');
 input.focus();
 setNewBlitzWord();
 input.addEventListener('input', () => {
 if (!bStarted) {
 bStarted = true;
 startBlitzTimer();
 }
 const val = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
 if (val === currentBlitzWord) {
 bWordsTyped++;
 bStreak++;
 if (bStreak % 5 === 0 && bMultiplier < 4) bMultiplier++;
 document.getElementById('blitz-words').textContent = bWordsTyped;
 document.getElementById('blitz-mult').textContent = `${bMultiplier}x`;
 input.value = '';
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 setNewBlitzWord();
 }
 });
});
function setNewBlitzWord() {
 currentBlitzWord = BLITZ_CORPUS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BLITZ_CORPUS.length)];
 document.getElementById('blitz-word').textContent = currentBlitzWord.toUpperCase();
}
function startBlitzTimer() {
 bInterval = setInterval(() => {
 bTimeRemaining--;
 document.getElementById('blitz-clock').textContent = `${bTimeRemaining}s`;
 if (bTimeRemaining <= 0) {
 clearInterval(bInterval);
 alert(`Time's up! You completed ${bWordsTyped} blitz words with a peak multiplier of ${bMultiplier}x!`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 }
 }, 1000);
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\games\zombie_outbreak.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:880px; margin:0 auto; text-align:center;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.25rem;">
 <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:800; margin:0;">🧟 Zombie Defense Outbreak</h2>
 <div style="display:flex; gap:1.2rem; align-items:center;">
 <span>Eliminated: <strong id="zombie-score" style="color:var(--accent); font-size:1.25rem;">0</strong></span>
 <span>Base Health: <strong id="zombie-health" style="color:var(--danger); font-size:1.25rem;">100%</strong></span>
 <a href="{{ url_for('games.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&times; Exit</a>
 </div>
 </div>
 <div id="zombie-arena" style="position:relative; width:100%; height:380px; background:var(--bg-secondary); border:2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius); overflow:hidden;">
 <div style="position:absolute; right:30px; top:0; bottom:0; width:4px; background:var(--accent); opacity:0.6;"></div>
 </div>
 <div style="margin-top:0.75rem; font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--text-muted);">
 Zombies approach your base from the left. Type their word cleanly to fire a laser bolt.
 </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const ZOMBIE_WORDS = ["bite", "crawl", "undead", "shadow", "stalker", "ghoul", "decay", "danger", "frenzy", "horde", "curse", "infect"];
let zScore = 0;
let zHealth = 100;
let zombies = [];
let zSpawnTimer = null;
let zMoveTimer = null;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 zSpawnTimer = setInterval(spawnZombie, 2200);
 zMoveTimer = setInterval(moveZombies, 50);
 window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
 if (zHealth <= 0) return;
 if (e.key.length !== 1 || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
 const char = e.key.toLowerCase();
 const candidate = zombies.find(z => z.text.startsWith(z.typed + char));
 if (candidate) {
 candidate.typed += char;
 candidate.el.innerHTML = `🧟 <span style="color:var(--success); text-decoration:underline;">${candidate.typed}</span>${candidate.text.slice(candidate.typed.length)}`;
 if (candidate.typed === candidate.text) {
 candidate.el.remove();
 zombies.splice(zombies.indexOf(candidate), 1);
 zScore++;
 document.getElementById('zombie-score').textContent = zScore;
 }
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
 } else {
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 }
 });
});
function spawnZombie() {
 const arena = document.getElementById('zombie-arena');
 if (!arena || zHealth <= 0) return;
 const text = ZOMBIE_WORDS[Math.floor(Math.random() * ZOMBIE_WORDS.length)];
 const el = document.createElement('div');
 el.innerHTML = `🧟 <span>${text}</span>`;
 el.style.position = 'absolute';
 el.style.left = '0px';
 el.style.top = `${Math.random() * (arena.clientHeight - 60) + 15}px`;
 el.style.padding = '0.35rem 0.65rem';
 el.style.background = 'var(--bg-card)';
 el.style.border = '1px solid var(--danger)';
 el.style.borderRadius = '4px';
 el.style.fontFamily = 'var(--font-mono)';
 el.style.fontWeight = '700';
 arena.appendChild(el);
 zombies.push({ el, text, typed: '', x: 0, speed: 1.2 + Math.random() });
}
function moveZombies() {
 const arena = document.getElementById('zombie-arena');
 if (!arena || zHealth <= 0) return;
 for (let i = zombies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
 const z = zombies[i];
 z.x += z.speed;
 z.el.style.left = `${z.x}px`;
 // Breach base barrier
 if (z.x >= arena.clientWidth - 110) {
 z.el.remove();
 zombies.splice(i, 1);
 zHealth -= 25;
 document.getElementById('zombie-health').textContent = `${zHealth}%`;
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(true);
 if (zHealth <= 0) {
 clearInterval(zSpawnTimer);
 clearInterval(zMoveTimer);
 alert(`Perimeter breached! You survived and eliminated ${zScore} zombies.`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('games.index') }}";
 break;
 }
 }
 }
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\learn\lesson.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width:880px; margin:0 auto;">
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom:1rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 <div>
 <span style="font-size:0.75rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--accent);">STAGE {{ stage.stage_number }} EXERCISE</span>
 <h2 style="font-size:1.5rem; font-weight:800; margin:0.2rem 0 0;">{{ stage.title }}</h2>
 </div>
 <a href="{{ url_for('learn.index') }}" class="btn btn-sm">&larr; Curriculum</a>
 </div>
 <p style="color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.9rem; line-height:1.5; margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 {{ stage.description }}
 </p>
 <div class="alert-box alert-info" style="margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
 Passing Requirements: Minimum <strong>{{ stage.min_wpm_to_pass|int }} WPM</strong> and <strong>{{ stage.min_accuracy_to_pass|int }}% Accuracy</strong>.
 </div>
 <!-- Lesson Arena -->
 <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3, 1fr); gap:0.75rem; margin-bottom:1.25rem;">
 <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">Live WPM</div><div class="stat-value" id="lesson-wpm">0</div></div>
 <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">Accuracy</div><div class="stat-value" id="lesson-acc">100%</div></div>
 <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">Errors</div><div class="stat-value" id="lesson-err" style="color:var(--danger);">0</div></div>
 </div>
 <div id="lesson-box" class="typing-container focus-ring" style="min-height:150px; max-height:220px; margin-bottom:1.25rem;">
 <div id="lesson-caret" class="caret caret-smooth"></div>
 <div id="lesson-text" class="typing-text-display"></div>
 </div>
 <input type="text" id="lesson-input" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Type the exercise above to begin..." style="width:100%; padding:0.85rem; background:var(--bg-card); border:1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:var(--radius-sm); color:var(--text-primary); font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:1.1rem; outline:none;">
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
const targetMaterial = {{ stage.practice_material|tojson }};
const stageId = {{ stage.id }};
const minWpm = {{ stage.min_wpm_to_pass }};
const minAcc = {{ stage.min_accuracy_to_pass }};
let startTime = null;
let currentIdx = 0;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 renderLessonText();
 const input = document.getElementById('lesson-input');
 input.focus();
 input.addEventListener('input', () => {
 if (!startTime) startTime = performance.now();
 const val = input.value;
 const spans = document.querySelectorAll('#lesson-text .char');
 currentIdx = Math.min(val.length, targetMaterial.length);
 let errors = 0;
 for (let i = 0; i < spans.length; i++) {
 if (i < val.length) {
 if (val[i] === targetMaterial[i]) {
 spans[i].className = 'char correct';
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char incorrect';
 errors++;
 }
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char';
 }
 }
 // Caret Position
 const caret = document.getElementById('lesson-caret');
 if (currentIdx < spans.length) {
 caret.style.left = `${spans[currentIdx].offsetLeft}px`;
 caret.style.top = `${spans[currentIdx].offsetTop + 4}px`;
 }
 // Telemetry
 const elapsedMinutes = (performance.now() - startTime) / 60000;
 const netWords = Math.max(0, (currentIdx - errors) / 5.0);
 const wpm = elapsedMinutes > 0 ? Math.round(netWords / elapsedMinutes) : 0;
 const acc = currentIdx > 0 ? Math.round(((currentIdx - errors) / currentIdx) * 100) : 100;
 document.getElementById('lesson-wpm').textContent = wpm;
 document.getElementById('lesson-acc').textContent = `${acc}%`;
 document.getElementById('lesson-err').textContent = errors;
 if (val === targetMaterial) {
 input.disabled = true;
 submitCompletion(wpm, acc);
 }
 });
});
function renderLessonText() {
 const container = document.getElementById('lesson-text');
 container.innerHTML = '';
 for (let i = 0; i < targetMaterial.length; i++) {
 const s = document.createElement('span');
 s.className = 'char';
 s.textContent = targetMaterial[i];
 container.appendChild(s);
 }
}
async function submitCompletion(wpm, acc) {
 const passed = (wpm >= minWpm && acc >= minAcc);
 try {
 await fetch('/learn/api/complete-stage', {
 method: 'POST',
 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 body: JSON.stringify({ stage_id: stageId, wpm: wpm, accuracy: acc })
 });
 } catch {}
 alert(passed 
 ? `🎉 Stage Passed! Speed: ${wpm} WPM | Accuracy: ${acc}%. Progression updated!`
 : `Stage Incomplete. Score: ${wpm} WPM (Target: ${minWpm}) & ${acc}% Acc. Try again!`);
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('learn.index') }}";
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script src="https://cdn.socket.io/4.7.2/socket.io.min.js"></script>
<script>
// ==========================================
// Master Multiplayer Controller
// ==========================================
let socket = null;
let mySid = null;
const isMobile = /Mobi|Android|iPhone|iPad/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
const platformTag = isMobile ? "Phone" : "PC";
const authUsername = "{{ current_user.username if current_user.is_authenticated else '' }}";
const myPlayerName = authUsername ? authUsername : `Pilot_${platformTag}_${Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900)}`;
let activeRoomData = null;
let currentPassage = "";
let currentInputIndex = 0;
let raceActive = false;
let isCountingDown = false;
let raceStartTime = null;
let clientRaceTimer = null;
let hasLocallyFinished = false;
let creationSelectedDur = 60;
let creationType = 'public';
const trackElements = {};
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 initSocket();
 const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
 const joinParam = params.get('join') || params.get('room');
 if (joinParam) {
 setTimeout(() => {
 socket.emit('join_room', { code: joinParam.trim().toUpperCase(), player_name: myPlayerName });
 }, 450);
 }
 bindArenaInput();
 highlightActiveNavPill('public');
});
function initSocket() {
 socket = io();
 socket.on('connect', () => {
 mySid = socket.id;
 document.getElementById('socket-conn-dot').style.background = 'var(--success)';
 document.getElementById('socket-conn-text').textContent = `Online as ${myPlayerName}`;
 requestPublicRooms();
 });
 socket.on('disconnect', () => {
 document.getElementById('socket-conn-dot').style.background = 'var(--danger)';
 document.getElementById('socket-conn-text').textContent = 'Disconnected. Reconnecting...';
 });
 socket.on('public_rooms_update', (data) => {
 renderPublicRooms(data.rooms || []);
 });
 socket.on('room_joined', (data) => {
 mySid = data.your_sid;
 activeRoomData = data.room;
 currentPassage = data.room.text;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 showView('waiting');
 renderWaitingLobby();
 });
 socket.on('room_state_updated', (data) => {
 activeRoomData = data.room;
 currentPassage = data.room.text;
 if (activeRoomData.status === 'waiting') {
 renderWaitingLobby();
 }
 });
 socket.on('join_error', (data) => {
 alert(data.message || 'Unable to join room.');
 });
 socket.on('action_error', (data) => {
 alert(data.message || 'Action failed.');
 });
 socket.on('quick_queue_waiting', () => {
 document.getElementById('modal-quick-searching').style.display = 'flex';
 });
 socket.on('quick_queue_cancelled', () => {
 document.getElementById('modal-quick-searching').style.display = 'none';
 });
 socket.on('quick_match_paired', (data) => {
 document.getElementById('modal-quick-searching').style.display = 'none';
 mySid = data.your_sid;
 activeRoomData = data.room;
 currentPassage = data.room.text;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 showView('waiting');
 renderWaitingLobby();
 });
 socket.on('match_countdown_started', (data) => {
 currentPassage = data.text;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 showView('arena');
 executeCountdown(data.duration || 60);
 });
 socket.on('room_progress_update', (data) => {
 renderArenaTracks(data.players || []);
 });
 socket.on('individual_player_finished', (data) => {
 const finishedPlayer = data.player;
 const isMe = (finishedPlayer.sid === mySid);
 if (isMe) {
 hasLocallyFinished = true;
 document.getElementById('finished-spectate-banner').style.display = 'flex';
 const medal = (finishedPlayer.place === 1) ? '🥇 1st' : ((finishedPlayer.place === 2) ? '🥈 2nd' : '🥉 3rd');
 document.getElementById('spectate-title').textContent = `🏁 You finished! Position: ${medal} (${Math.round(finishedPlayer.wpm)} WPM)`;
 document.getElementById('spectate-subtext').textContent = data.remaining_count > 0 
 ? `${data.remaining_count} racer(s) still on track. You are now spectating live.`
 : 'All racers finished!';
 }
 if (data.all_finished) {
 setTimeout(() => {
 showResultsView(data.standings);
 }, 900);
 }
 });
 socket.on('race_concluded', (data) => {
 clearInterval(clientRaceTimer);
 setTimeout(() => {
 showResultsView(data.standings);
 }, 900);
 });
 socket.on('rematch_accepted', (data) => {
 activeRoomData = data.room;
 currentPassage = data.room.text;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 showView('waiting');
 renderWaitingLobby();
 });
 socket.on('left_room_confirmed', () => {
 activeRoomData = null;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 clearInterval(clientRaceTimer);
 showView('hub');
 requestPublicRooms();
 });
}
// ==========================================
// Mode Switching & Safety Guards
// ==========================================
function switchModeTab(targetMode) {
 if (activeRoomData && (activeRoomData.status === 'in_progress' || activeRoomData.status === 'countdown') && !hasLocallyFinished) {
 const confirmLeave = confirm("You are currently inside an active race! Switching modes will record you as DNF. Continue?");
 if (!confirmLeave) return;
 }
 if (activeRoomData) {
 socket.emit('leave_room_voluntary');
 }
 cancelQuickMatchQueue();
 highlightActiveNavPill(targetMode);
 showView('hub');
 if (targetMode === 'quick') {
 startQuickMatchQueue();
 } else if (targetMode === 'private') {
 openCreateModal('private');
 } else {
 const dir = document.getElementById('public-directory-section');
 if (dir) dir.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
 requestPublicRooms();
 }
}
function highlightActiveNavPill(activeMode) {
 const pPub = document.getElementById('tab-pill-public');
 const pQck = document.getElementById('tab-pill-quick');
 const pPrv = document.getElementById('tab-pill-private');
 pPub.className = (activeMode === 'public') ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 pQck.className = (activeMode === 'quick') ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 pPrv.className = (activeMode === 'private') ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn';
 const cPub = document.getElementById('card-mode-public');
 const cQck = document.getElementById('card-mode-quick');
 const cPrv = document.getElementById('card-mode-private');
 if (cPub) cPub.style.borderColor = (activeMode === 'public') ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border-color)';
 if (cQck) cQck.style.borderColor = (activeMode === 'quick') ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border-color)';
 if (cPrv) cPrv.style.borderColor = (activeMode === 'private') ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border-color)';
}
function showView(viewName) {
 document.getElementById('view-hub').style.display = (viewName === 'hub') ? 'block' : 'none';
 document.getElementById('view-waiting').style.display = (viewName === 'waiting') ? 'block' : 'none';
 document.getElementById('view-arena').style.display = (viewName === 'arena') ? 'block' : 'none';
 document.getElementById('view-results').style.display = (viewName === 'results') ? 'block' : 'none';
}
function safeLeaveRoom() {
 if (activeRoomData && (activeRoomData.status === 'in_progress' || activeRoomData.status === 'countdown') && !hasLocallyFinished) {
 if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to abandon this race?")) return;
 }
 socket.emit('leave_room_voluntary');
}
function leaveEarlyAfterFinish() {
 socket.emit('leave_race_after_finish');
}
// ==========================================
// Hub Directory
// ==========================================
function requestPublicRooms() {
 if (socket && socket.connected) {
 socket.emit('request_public_rooms');
 }
}
function renderPublicRooms(rooms) {
 const tbody = document.getElementById('public-rooms-tbody');
 if (!tbody) return;
 tbody.innerHTML = '';
 if (rooms.length === 0) {
 tbody.innerHTML = `
 <tr>
 <td colspan="7" style="padding: 2.5rem; text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted);">
 No public rooms waiting. Click <strong>"+ Create Public Room"</strong> to host!
 </td>
 </tr>
 `;
 return;
 }
 rooms.forEach(r => {
 const tr = document.createElement('tr');
 tr.innerHTML = `
 <td style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent);">${r.code}</td>
 <td style="font-weight: 600;">${r.name}</td>
 <td>${r.host_name}</td>
 <td>${r.current_players} / ${r.max_players}</td>
 <td>${r.duration}s</td>
 <td>
 <span style="background: var(--success-bg); color: var(--success); padding: 0.2rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700;">WAITING</span>
 </td>
 <td>
 <button onclick="joinRoomByCode('${r.code}')" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">
 Join Room
 </button>
 </td>
 `;
 tbody.appendChild(tr);
 });
}
function joinRoomByCode(code) {
 socket.emit('join_room', { code: code, player_name: myPlayerName });
}
function joinRoomByCodeInput() {
 const input = document.getElementById('join-code-input');
 const code = input.value.trim().toUpperCase();
 if (!code) {
 alert('Please enter a room code.');
 return;
 }
 joinRoomByCode(code);
}
function startQuickMatchQueue() {
 socket.emit('join_quick_queue', { name: myPlayerName });
}
function cancelQuickMatchQueue() {
 socket.emit('cancel_quick_queue');
 highlightActiveNavPill('public');
}
function openCreateModal(type) {
 creationType = type;
 document.getElementById('create-modal-title').textContent = (type === 'private') ? "Create Private 1v1 Room" : "Create Public Room";
 document.getElementById('create-players-wrap').style.display = (type === 'private') ? 'none' : 'block';
 document.getElementById('modal-create-room').style.display = 'flex';
}
function closeCreateModal() {
 document.getElementById('modal-create-room').style.display = 'none';
}
function selectCreateDur(dur, btn) {
 creationSelectedDur = dur;
 document.querySelectorAll('#create-dur-group .btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active-choice'));
 btn.classList.add('active-choice');
}
function submitCreateRoom() {
 const name = document.getElementById('create-room-name').value.trim() || 'Speed Duel';
 const maxP = (creationType === 'private') ? 2 : parseInt(document.getElementById('create-max-players').value, 10);
 const customText = document.getElementById('create-custom-text').value.trim();
 socket.emit('create_room', {
 type: creationType,
 name: name,
 player_name: myPlayerName,
 duration: creationSelectedDur,
 max_players: maxP,
 custom_text: customText
 });
 closeCreateModal();
}
// ==========================================
// Waiting Room / Lobby
// ==========================================
function renderWaitingLobby() {
 if (!activeRoomData) return;
 document.getElementById('waiting-room-code').textContent = activeRoomData.code;
 document.getElementById('waiting-room-type').textContent = activeRoomData.type.toUpperCase();
 document.getElementById('waiting-room-dur').textContent = `${activeRoomData.duration}s`;
 const container = document.getElementById('waiting-racers-list');
 container.innerHTML = '';
 const players = Object.values(activeRoomData.players || {}).filter(p => !p.left_early);
 let allReady = true;
 players.forEach(p => {
 const isMe = (p.sid === mySid);
 if (!p.ready) allReady = false;
 const div = document.createElement('div');
 div.style.display = 'flex';
 div.style.justifyContent = 'space-between';
 div.style.alignItems = 'center';
 div.style.background = isMe ? 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12)' : 'var(--bg-secondary)';
 div.style.border = isMe ? '1.5px solid var(--accent)' : '1px solid var(--border-color)';
 div.style.padding = '0.75rem 1rem';
 div.style.borderRadius = 'var(--radius-sm)';
 div.innerHTML = `
 <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.6rem;">
 <span style="font-size: 1.15rem;">🏎️</span>
 <strong style="font-size: 0.95rem;">${isMe ? `You (${p.name})` : p.name}</strong>
 ${p.is_host ? '<span style="font-size: 0.7rem; background: var(--accent); color: #04101e; padding: 0.1rem 0.45rem; border-radius: 4px; font-weight: 700;">HOST</span>' : ''}
 </div>
 <div>
 <span style="font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: 700; padding: 0.25rem 0.65rem; border-radius: 4px; background: ${p.ready ? 'var(--success-bg)' : 'var(--warning-bg)'}; color: ${p.ready ? 'var(--success)' : 'var(--warning)'};">
 ${p.ready ? '✓ Ready' : '⏳ Not Ready'}
 </span>
 </div>
 `;
 container.appendChild(div);
 });
 const amHost = (activeRoomData.host_sid === mySid);
 const hostBtn = document.getElementById('btn-host-start');
 const readyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-ready');
 if (amHost) {
 hostBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
 const canStart = (players.length >= 2 || activeRoomData.type === 'public') && allReady;
 hostBtn.disabled = !canStart;
 hostBtn.style.opacity = canStart ? '1' : '0.5';
 } else {
 hostBtn.style.display = 'none';
 }
 const myRecord = activeRoomData.players[mySid];
 if (myRecord) {
 readyBtn.textContent = myRecord.ready ? 'Cancel Ready' : 'Mark Ready (✓)';
 readyBtn.className = myRecord.ready ? 'btn' : 'btn btn-primary';
 }
}
function copyLobbyInvite() {
 if (!activeRoomData) return;
 const link = `${window.location.origin}/multiplayer/?join=${encodeURIComponent(activeRoomData.code)}`;
 navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(() => {
 const btn = document.getElementById('btn-copy-lobby');
 btn.textContent = 'Link Copied! ✓';
 btn.style.color = 'var(--success)';
 setTimeout(() => {
 btn.textContent = '📋 Copy Link';
 btn.style.color = '';
 }, 2000);
 });
}
function toggleMyReadyStatus() {
 socket.emit('toggle_ready');
}
function hostStartMatch() {
 socket.emit('start_match');
}
function requestRematch() {
 socket.emit('rematch_request');
}
// ==========================================
// Race Arena Execution & Smooth Auto-Centering
// ==========================================
function executeCountdown(durationSec) {
 isCountingDown = true;
 raceActive = false;
 currentInputIndex = 0;
 raceStartTime = null;
 hasLocallyFinished = false;
 document.getElementById('finished-spectate-banner').style.display = 'none';
 const banner = document.getElementById('arena-countdown-banner');
 const num = document.getElementById('arena-countdown-num');
 const statusBadge = document.getElementById('arena-status-badge');
 const input = document.getElementById('arena-cockpit-input');
 banner.style.display = 'block';
 statusBadge.textContent = 'Standby for countdown...';
 input.value = '';
 input.disabled = true;
 renderArenaPassage();
 let count = 3;
 num.textContent = count;
 const cdTimer = setInterval(() => {
 count--;
 if (count > 0) {
 num.textContent = count;
 } else if (count === 0) {
 num.textContent = 'GO!';
 num.style.color = 'var(--success)';
 statusBadge.textContent = 'RACE ACTIVE!';
 raceActive = true;
 isCountingDown = false;
 raceStartTime = performance.now();
 socket.emit('client_race_active');
 input.disabled = false;
 input.focus();
 startClientRaceTimer(durationSec);
 } else {
 clearInterval(cdTimer);
 banner.style.display = 'none';
 num.style.color = 'var(--accent)';
 }
 }, 1000);
}
function startClientRaceTimer(durationSec) {
 clearInterval(clientRaceTimer);
 let elapsed = 0;
 clientRaceTimer = setInterval(() => {
 if (!raceActive && hasLocallyFinished) return;
 elapsed++;
 const remaining = Math.max(0, durationSec - elapsed);
 if (remaining > 0) {
 document.getElementById('arena-status-badge').textContent = `Time Remaining: ${remaining}s`;
 } else {
 clearInterval(clientRaceTimer);
 socket.emit('time_expired_finish');
 }
 }, 1000);
}
function renderArenaPassage() {
 const display = document.getElementById('arena-passage-text');
 display.innerHTML = '';
 for (let i = 0; i < currentPassage.length; i++) {
 const span = document.createElement('span');
 span.className = 'char';
 span.textContent = currentPassage[i];
 display.appendChild(span);
 }
 updateArenaCaret();
}
function updateArenaCaret() {
 const spans = document.querySelectorAll('#arena-passage-text .char');
 const caret = document.getElementById('arena-caret');
 const box = document.getElementById('arena-passage-box');
 if (!caret || !box) return;
 if (currentInputIndex < spans.length) {
 const target = spans[currentInputIndex];
 caret.style.display = 'block';
 caret.style.left = `${target.offsetLeft}px`;
 caret.style.top = `${target.offsetTop + 4}px`;
 const targetMid = target.offsetTop - (box.clientHeight / 2) + 25;
 if (Math.abs(box.scrollTop - targetMid) > 20) {
 box.scrollTo({
 top: Math.max(0, targetMid),
 behavior: 'smooth'
 });
 }
 } else if (spans.length > 0) {
 const last = spans[spans.length - 1];
 caret.style.left = `${last.offsetLeft + last.offsetWidth}px`;
 caret.style.top = `${last.offsetTop + 4}px`;
 }
}
function renderArenaTracks(players) {
 const container = document.getElementById('arena-tracks-container');
 players.forEach(p => {
 let track = trackElements[p.sid];
 const isMe = (p.sid === mySid);
 const label = isMe ? `🏎️ <strong>You</strong> <span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.75rem;">(${p.name})</span>` : `🏎️ <strong>${p.name}</strong>`;
 if (!track) {
 track = document.createElement('div');
 track.id = `arena-track-${p.sid}`;
 track.style.background = 'var(--bg-card)';
 track.style.padding = '0.75rem 1rem';
 track.style.borderRadius = 'var(--radius-sm)';
 track.style.border = isMe ? '1.5px solid var(--accent)' : '1px solid var(--border-color)';
 track.innerHTML = `
 <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:0.85rem; margin-bottom:0.35rem;">
 <span>${label}</span>
 <span class="trk-speed-text" style="font-family:var(--font-mono); font-weight:700; color:${isMe ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--text-primary)'};">0 WPM (0%)</span>
 </div>
 <div style="background:var(--bg-secondary); height:10px; border-radius:5px; overflow:hidden;">
 <div class="trk-speed-bar" style="width:0%; height:100%; background:${isMe ? 'var(--accent)' : '#94a3b8'}; transition:width 0.15s ease;"></div>
 </div>
 `;
 container.appendChild(track);
 trackElements[p.sid] = track;
 }
 const rankSuffix = p.place ? ` [Finished #${p.place}]` : '';
 track.querySelector('.trk-speed-text').textContent = `${Math.round(p.wpm || 0)} WPM (${Math.round(p.progress || 0)}%)${rankSuffix}`;
 const bar = track.querySelector('.trk-speed-bar');
 bar.style.width = `${p.progress || 0}%`;
 if (p.progress >= 100) {
 bar.style.background = 'var(--success)';
 }
 });
 const currentSids = new Set(players.map(p => p.sid));
 Object.keys(trackElements).forEach(sid => {
 if (!currentSids.has(sid)) {
 trackElements[sid].remove();
 delete trackElements[sid];
 }
 });
}
function bindArenaInput() {
 const input = document.getElementById('arena-cockpit-input');
 input.addEventListener('input', () => {
 if (!raceActive || hasLocallyFinished) {
 input.value = '';
 return;
 }
 const val = input.value;
 const spans = document.querySelectorAll('#arena-passage-text .char');
 currentInputIndex = Math.min(val.length, currentPassage.length);
 let mistakes = 0;
 for (let i = 0; i < spans.length; i++) {
 if (i < val.length) {
 if (val[i] === currentPassage[i]) {
 spans[i].className = 'char correct';
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char incorrect';
 mistakes++;
 }
 } else {
 spans[i].className = 'char';
 }
 }
 updateArenaCaret();
 if (val.length > 0) {
 const lastChar = val[val.length - 1];
 const isCorrect = (val.length <= currentPassage.length) && (lastChar === currentPassage[val.length - 1]);
 if (window.soundEngine) {
 window.soundEngine.playKey(!isCorrect);
 }
 }
 const progress = Math.min(100, Math.round((currentInputIndex / currentPassage.length) * 100));
 const elapsedMin = (performance.now() - raceStartTime) / 60000;
 const netWords = Math.max(0, (currentInputIndex - mistakes) / 5.0);
 const wpm = elapsedMin > 0 ? Math.round(netWords / elapsedMin) : 0;
 const accuracy = currentInputIndex > 0 ? Math.round(((currentInputIndex - mistakes) / currentInputIndex) * 100) : 100;
 socket.emit('progress_update', {
 progress: progress,
 wpm: wpm,
 accuracy: accuracy,
 errors: mistakes
 });
 if (val === currentPassage) {
 hasLocallyFinished = true;
 raceActive = false;
 input.blur();
 }
 });
 input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
 if (e.key === ' ' && !raceActive) {
 e.preventDefault();
 }
 });
}
// ==========================================
// Results Screen & Contextual Feedback
// ==========================================
function showResultsView(standings) {
 showView('results');
 const tbody = document.getElementById('results-standings-tbody');
 tbody.innerHTML = '';
 const winner = standings[0];
 const isWinnerMe = (winner && winner.sid === mySid);
 const trophyIcon = document.getElementById('results-trophy-icon');
 const titleEl = document.getElementById('results-headline-title');
 const bannerEl = document.getElementById('results-motivational-banner');
 if (isWinnerMe) {
 trophyIcon.textContent = "🏆";
 titleEl.textContent = "VICTORY! Outstanding Speed!";
 titleEl.style.color = "var(--warning)";
 bannerEl.style.borderColor = "var(--warning)";
 bannerEl.style.background = "var(--warning-bg)";
 bannerEl.innerHTML = `🥇 <strong>First Place Champion!</strong> You dominated the grid with unmatched precision!`;
 } else {
 trophyIcon.textContent = "🏁";
 titleEl.textContent = "Race Concluded";
 titleEl.style.color = "var(--accent)";
 bannerEl.style.borderColor = "var(--accent)";
 bannerEl.style.background = "var(--accent-glow)";
 const myIndex = standings.findIndex(p => p.sid === mySid);
 const myRank = myIndex + 1;
 let motivation = "Great effort! Every race sharpens your tactile muscle memory.";
 if (myRank === 2) {
 motivation = "🥈 Silver finish! You were so close to 1st place — keep this momentum!";
 } else if (myRank === 3) {
 motivation = "🥉 Podium finish! Solid precision, keep pushing to close the gap!";
 } else {
 motivation = "Don't give up! Challenge the winner to a rematch and take back the lead.";
 }
 bannerEl.innerHTML = `You placed <strong>#${myRank > 0 ? myRank : 'DNF'}</strong> &bull; ${motivation}`;
 }
 standings.forEach((p, idx) => {
 const isMe = (p.sid === mySid);
 const tr = document.createElement('tr');
 if (isMe) {
 tr.style.background = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.1)';
 }
 const medal = (idx === 0) ? '🥇 1st' : ((idx === 1) ? '🥈 2nd' : ((idx === 2) ? '🥉 3rd' : `${idx + 1}th`));
 const statusTag = p.left_early 
 ? '<span style="color:var(--danger); font-weight:700;">DNF / Left</span>' 
 : (p.finished ? '<span style="color:var(--success); font-weight:700;">Finished</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--warning); font-weight:700;">Racing</span>');
 tr.innerHTML = `
 <td style="font-weight: 800; color: ${idx === 0 ? 'var(--warning)' : 'var(--text-primary)'};">${medal}</td>
 <td style="font-weight: 700;">${isMe ? `You (${p.name})` : p.name}</td>
 <td style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 800; color: var(--accent);">${Math.round(p.wpm || 0)} WPM</td>
 <td>${Math.round(p.accuracy || 100)}%</td>
 <td style="color: var(--danger); font-weight: 700;">${p.errors || 0}</td>
 <td>${statusTag}</td>
 `;
 tbody.appendChild(tr);
 });
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
function selectTheme(t) { window.settingsManager.update('theme', t); }
function selectFont(f) { window.settingsManager.update('font_family', f); }
function selectFontSize(s) {
 document.getElementById('font-size-label').textContent = s + 'px';
 window.settingsManager.update('font_size', parseInt(s, 10));
}
function selectCaret(c) { window.settingsManager.update('caret_style', c); }
function selectSound(s) {
 window.settingsManager.update('sound_theme', s);
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(false);
}
function handleSandboxKey(e) {
 if (window.soundEngine) window.soundEngine.playKey(e.key === 'Backspace');
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

### app\templates\typing\custom.html ###
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card" style="max-width: 840px; margin: 0 auto;">
 <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color); padding-bottom: 1.25rem; margin-bottom: 2rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1rem;">
 <div>
 <h1 style="font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: 700; margin: 0;">Custom Text Practice Studio</h1>
 <p style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 0.35rem;">
 Paste your text, choose your timer countdown, and launch your personalized benchmark.
 </p>
 </div>
 <a href="{{ url_for('typing.test_page') }}" class="btn">&larr; Return to Test Track</a>
 </div>
 <!-- Real-time Text Telemetry -->
 <div class="stats-hud" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
 <div class="stat-box">
 <div class="stat-label">Word Count</div>
 <div class="stat-value" id="stat-word-count">0</div>
 </div>
 <div class="stat-box">
 <div class="stat-label">Character Count</div>
 <div class="stat-value" id="stat-char-count">0</div>
 </div>
 <div class="stat-box">
 <div class="stat-label">Est. Time (@ 60 WPM)</div>
 <div class="stat-value" id="stat-est-time">0s</div>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Duration Mode Selection -->
 <div style="margin-bottom: 1.5rem; background: var(--bg-card); padding: 1rem 1.25rem; border-radius: var(--radius); border: 1px solid var(--border-color);">
 <label style="display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-primary); margin-bottom: 0.5rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em;">
 Select Test Timer (Countdown Limit)
 </label>
 <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.75rem; flex-wrap: wrap;" id="duration-selector">
 <button type="button" class="btn active-choice" onclick="selectDuration(60, this)" data-tooltip="Timer will start at 60s and count down to 0, automatically finishing test">1 Minute (60s Countdown)</button>
 <button type="button" class="btn" onclick="selectDuration(120, this)" data-tooltip="Timer will start at 120s and count down to 0">2 Minutes (120s Countdown)</button>
 <button type="button" class="btn" onclick="selectDuration(180, this)" data-tooltip="Timer will start at 180s and count down to 0">3 Minutes (180s Countdown)</button>
 <button type="button" class="btn" onclick="selectDuration(0, this)" data-tooltip="No countdown limit. Test runs until you type the entire custom paragraph">Full Passage (No Time Limit)</button>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Main Text Input -->
 <div style="margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
 <label style="display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem; color: var(--text-muted); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em;">
 Paste Content Below
 </label>
 <textarea id="custom-studio-input" rows="8" placeholder="Paste your custom study text, lyrics, or programming code here..." oninput="updateStats()" style="width: 100%; padding: 1rem; background: var(--bg-card); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: var(--radius); color: var(--text-primary); font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.6; outline: none;"></textarea>
 </div>
 <!-- Quick Presets -->
 <div style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">
 <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Or load a curated preset:</div>
 <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap;">
 <button onclick="loadPreset('tech')" class="btn" style="padding: 0.3rem 0.65rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">Distributed Systems</button>
 <button onclick="loadPreset('lit')" class="btn" style="padding: 0.3rem 0.65rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">Classic Literature</button>
 <button onclick="loadPreset('code')" class="btn" style="padding: 0.3rem 0.65rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">Python Quicksort</button>
 </div>
 </div>
 <!-- Action Button -->
 <div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 0.75rem;">
 <button onclick="clearText()" class="btn">Clear</button>
 <button onclick="launchCustomTest()" class="btn btn-primary" style="padding: 0.75rem 2rem; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 700;">
 Launch Custom Practice Track &rarr;
 </button>
 </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
let selectedCustomDuration = 60; // default 1 min
const PRESETS = {
 tech: "Distributed event streaming architectures enable microservices to communicate asynchronously with high throughput and horizontal scalability. By decoupling producers from consumers through immutable partitioned logs, systems achieve resilience against node failures.",
 lit: "It was the best of times, it was the worst of times, it was the age of wisdom, it was the age of foolishness, it was the epoch of belief, it was the epoch of incredulity, it was the season of light, it was the season of darkness.",
 code: "def binary_search(array, target):\n low, high = 0, len(array) - 1\n while low <= high:\n mid = (low + high) // 2\n if array[mid] == target:\n return mid\n elif array[mid] < target:\n low = mid + 1\n else:\n high = mid - 1\n return -1"
};
function selectDuration(seconds, btn) {
 selectedCustomDuration = seconds;
 document.querySelectorAll('#duration-selector .btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active-choice'));
 btn.classList.add('active-choice');
}
function updateStats() {
 const text = document.getElementById('custom-studio-input').value.trim();
 const words = text ? text.split(/\s+/).length : 0;
 const chars = text.length;
 const estSeconds = Math.round((words / 60) * 60);
 document.getElementById('stat-word-count').textContent = words;
 document.getElementById('stat-char-count').textContent = chars;
 document.getElementById('stat-est-time').textContent = `${estSeconds}s`;
}
function loadPreset(key) {
 document.getElementById('custom-studio-input').value = PRESETS[key];
 updateStats();
}
function clearText() {
 document.getElementById('custom-studio-input').value = '';
 updateStats();
}
function launchCustomTest() {
 const text = document.getElementById('custom-studio-input').value.trim();
 if (!text || text.length < 5) {
 alert('Please enter at least 5 characters to start a custom typing test.');
 return;
 }
 sessionStorage.setItem('typesphere_custom_text', text);
 sessionStorage.setItem('typesphere_custom_duration', selectedCustomDuration.toString());
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('typing.test_page') }}?mode=custom";
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 updateStats();
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
 try {
 const rawData = {{ test.timeline_data|tojson|safe if test.timeline_data else '[]' }};
 const timelineData = typeof rawData === 'string' ? JSON.parse(rawData) : rawData;
 if (Array.isArray(timelineData) && timelineData.length > 1) {
 SvgLineChart.render('chart-timeline', timelineData, '#38bdf8');
 }
 } catch {}
});
function toggleDetails() {
 const panel = document.getElementById('detailed-diagnostics-panel');
 const btn = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-details');
 if (panel.style.display === 'none') {
 panel.style.display = 'grid';
 btn.textContent = 'Hide Detailed Diagnostics ↑';
 } else {
 panel.style.display = 'none';
 btn.textContent = 'View Detailed Diagnostics ↓';
 }
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/keyboard.js') }}"></script>
<script src="{{ url_for('static', filename='js/engine.js') }}"></script>
<script>
const HINT_DESCRIPTIONS = {
 'easy': 'Easy Level: Short, high-frequency words, simple sentence structures, and minimal punctuation.',
 'moderate': 'Moderate Level: Standard vocabulary, common punctuation, and natural sentence structures.',
 'hard': 'Hard Level: Advanced vocabulary, longer words, complex sentence structures, and increased punctuation.',
 'expert': 'Expert Level: Sophisticated vocabulary, technical terminology, complex punctuation, and rapid reaches.'
};
function onTypeChange(typeVal) {
 if (typeVal === 'custom') {
 window.location.href = "{{ url_for('typing.custom_text_page') }}";
 return;
 }
 window.typingEngine.contentType = typeVal;
 window.typingEngine.loadPrompt(false);
}
function onTimeChange(timeVal) {
 const dur = parseInt(timeVal, 10);
 window.typingEngine.durationLimit = dur;
 window.typingEngine.loadPrompt(false);
}
function onDifficultyChange(diffVal) {
 window.typingEngine.level = diffVal;
 const hint = document.getElementById('config-explainer-hint');
 if (hint && HINT_DESCRIPTIONS[diffVal]) {
 hint.textContent = HINT_DESCRIPTIONS[diffVal];
 }
 window.typingEngine.loadPrompt(false);
}
function toggleBlindQuick() {
 const next = !window.typingEngine.blindModeActive;
 window.typingEngine.blindModeActive = next;
 const btn = document.getElementById('btn-blind');
 btn.textContent = `Blind: ${next ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;
 btn.classList.toggle('active-choice', next);
}
function toggleGhostQuick() {
 const next = !window.typingEngine.ghostEnabled;
 window.typingEngine.ghostEnabled = next;
 const btn = document.getElementById('btn-ghost');
 btn.textContent = `Ghost: ${next ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;
 btn.classList.toggle('active-choice', next);
}
function toggleAdvancedAnalyticsDrawer() {
 const drawer = document.getElementById('advanced-analytics-drawer');
 const btn = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-advanced');
 if (drawer.style.display === 'none') {
 drawer.style.display = 'block';
 btn.textContent = 'Hide Advanced Telemetry ↑';
 drawer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
 } else {
 drawer.style.display = 'none';
 btn.textContent = '📊 View Advanced Telemetry & Heatmap ↓';
 }
}
</script>
{% endblock %}
